# Jaquar - Global Bath Catalogue 2025-2026
### Complete catalogue extraction for Global Classic Building Material LLC

**Source:** Jaquar_Global_Catalogue_2025-2026_Web.pdf - "GLOBAL BATH CATALOGUE 2025-2026", global.jaquar.com, 364 pages (A4), print reference "CRESCENT- (GLOBAL BATH CATALOGUE) 05/2025" (p364). PDF produced May 2025 (Adobe InDesign 20.3, compressed via iLovePDF).
**Extraction method:** Full text layer extracted per page (pdftotext layout mode plus PyMuPDF block parsing); every SKU caption block was geometrically matched to its adjacent product image; graphical spec tables (whirlpool spec sheet p300-301, warranty table p360, spa feature sheet p334) were rasterized and transcribed visually. No pages were unreadable. The source is a web-compressed PDF: product cut-out images are embedded at roughly 150 dpi (typically 200-500 px wide), which is why the image pack must go through the Higgsfield upscale pass before shipping (see the build brief).
**Scope of this document:** This PDF covers Faucets, Shower Valves and Sanitaryware; i-Flush and Flushing Systems; Accessories; Showers (overhead, hand, body, panels, shower accessories); and Wellness (whirlpools, freestanding and built-in bathtubs, bathtub accessories, spas, saunas, steam solutions, water heaters, washroom accessories). It does NOT cover: shower enclosures (the site's entire shower-enclosures category - Ritz, frameless, framed, sliding, shower trays - is absent from this catalogue), Octane showers (live on the site, absent here), Jaquar Lighting, Artize or Essco product lines, and it prints NO PRICES.

**PRICES: none are printed anywhere in this catalogue.** The only price-related line is the warranty disclaimer "Jaquar has an ongoing policy of design, development and improvement and thus reserves the right to modify specification and prices without any prior notice." (p361). The SEO plan item "prices are the biggest gap in the UAE SERP" cannot be served from this document; a separate UAE price list is needed.

**Total products extracted: 1484** across 84 collections/ranges. Category totals: faucets 835, sanitary-ware 220, showers 123, flushing-systems 42, accessories 96, whirlpools 37, bathtubs 45, wellness 36, water-heaters 38, washroom-accessories 12. These counts reconcile row-for-row with `jaquar-catalogue-images/index.csv`.

## 2. Faucets, Shower Valves & Sanitaryware  (pages 36-243)

The PDF's own first category ("FAUCETS, SHOWER VALVES & SANITARYWARE", TOC p35). Collections in the PDF's order. Site mapping: entries feed both the live faucets and sanitary-ware categories; each block below is folder-mapped.

**How Jaquar finish codes work (as printed, p40/p56 and repeated on every finish card):** "If you wish to place orders for the different colour finishes of this range shown above, just replace the colour code in the middle of the product code with the colour code of your choice. For instance, in the product code KUP-CHR-35011BPM, 'CHR' notes Chrome Finish. If you wish to order the same product in Black Matt, replace 'CHR' with 'BLM'. Thus the product code will be KUP-BLM-35011BPM." Also on every card: "The finishes depicted here are only indicative and may differ from that on the actual product." This is the engine for the site's SKU decoder feature.

NOTE ON SKUs IN THE TABLES: products are printed in base form without the finish infix (e.g. LAG-91011B); the orderable code inserts the finish code (LAG-CHR-91011B). Sanitaryware SKUs carry the finish inline (LAS-WHT-91801, WHT = White). Flow rates marked with * are "at 3 bar pressure" (footnote printed on faucet pages, e.g. p41).

### Category tech notes - Faucets & Sanitaryware
- Bidspa feature list as printed (p207): Futuristic design; Cutting edge technology; Touch free; Smart button; UV nozzle; LED light; Adjustable temperature-water, air & seat; Smart deodorization; Automatic flushing; Bidet & rear wash; Self cleaning; 4.8L average flushing volume; Seat thickness is 6.5cm; Smart flushing system with brushless motor; Slim in shape; Powerful flushing with low noise; Automatic opening seat & cover; Foot sensor; Remote operated. Plus "Multi-functional remote control" and "Hygienic anti-bacterial surface".
- Sensor faucets are split "Battery Operated" and "With 9v Transformer | Electronic Operated" (pp176-180) - an installation-requirement fact worth a FAQ.
- Sanitaryware seat codes recurring in SKUs: UF (UF Seat Cover), PP (PP Seat Cover), SM (soft close-type marker as printed in descriptions), P180/S220/S300 trap distances (e.g. P-trap 180 mm, S-trap 220/300 mm) - exact meanings should be quoted from descriptions per product, not invented.

## 3. i-Flush & Flushing Systems  (pages 217-228)

**Category claim, verbatim (p219):** "Installed directly in the pipeline, the i Flush concealed flushing mechanism has multiple aesthetic and design advantages. It also provides a more appropriate mechanism of flushing, using the available water pressure to create better siphoning at the trap and superior clearing of waste."

**i-Flush advantages as printed (p219):** Repeated flushing is possible, whereas concealed cisterns will definitely take time to fill up after flushing once; i-Flush 32 mm can work efficiently at even low pressure (minimum 0.8 bar dynamics); Less noise in flushing; Dual flush - Saves water, a green product; Easy maintenance, control stop valve can be closed with the help of a T-type key provided along with the product; Access to control cartridge by removing front actuation plate hence easy accessibility for maintenance.

**Flushing Systems advantages as printed (p225):** "Saves Water: Impressive Dual Flush with a provision of 3/6 Ltr flush (Can be adjusted to 2/4 Ltrs)"; "Less Noise: Technically advanced, silent flush valve and fast-filling hydraulic float valve minimises the flushing sound during usage"; "Perfect Pan Cleaning: Heavy and even water discharge during every flush at every water pressure"; "No Damage from Condensation: Has anti-condensation lining wrapping the tank and supply bend"; Floor standing frame features: load "up to 400 kg", fixed inlet/outlet distance "135 mm from centre to centre", adjustable legs "(0-200 mm)", "no chiselling of the wall is required".

## 5. Showers  (pages 244-281)

**Category claims, verbatim (p245):** "Our advanced showers are designed to deliver great precision and consistent water distribution to each individual nozzle. Jaquar showers give you a choice of up to 5 different flows matching your mood." The five flows as printed: Normal, Soft, Massage, Cascade, Mist.

**Shower technologies as printed (p245):**
- Advanced Rubit Technology: "the spray jets on the showers are made from elastic silicone, they ensure that no limescale deposits stick to the shower face. Even if they do, they can be easily rubbed off by hand or flannel." (CONFIRMS the site's existing Rubit fact.)
- Click Position System: "You can feel and hear the click while changing the function of the shower sprays."
- Jaquar Booster Technology: "creates the sensation of a full shower regardless of the water pressure." (CONFIRMS the site's Booster fact.)
- Thermo Insulation: "Jaquar's insulated shower systems prevent scalding by not heating up the shower body."

**Shapes and sizes table as printed (p246):** Shapes: Square, Round, Oval, Rectangular. Hand Shower: 75 mm dia, 80 mm dia, 95 mm dia, 100 mm dia, 105 mm dia, 120 mm dia, 140 mm dia, 180 mm dia. Overhead Shower (Square): 150 x 150 mm, 200 x 200 mm, 240 x 240 mm, 300 x 300 mm, 450 x 450 mm - Registered design no.276017. Overhead Shower (Round): 80, 100, 105, 120, 140, 150, 180, 190, 200, 240, 300 mm dia - Registered design no.276016. Overhead Shower (Oval): 340 x 220 mm - Registered design no.276015. Overhead Shower (Round Edge & Rectangular head): 200 x 300 mm, 450 x 450 mm - Registered design no.276014. Overhead Shower (Rectangular head): 552 X 205 X 25 mm, 190 X 295 mm.

**Duoflo (p247):** "ENJOY A CHOICE OF TWO FLOWS TOGETHER." Features: Stainless steel AISI 304 body; "Remote-operated healing Chromotherapy light" *(printed as "healing" - kept verbatim)*; Available in Round and Square shapes; 2-functions in each - Rain + Mist or Rain + Waterfall; "Patented inverted riveting technology for leak proof showering".

**Hydrolite (p257):** "A light emitting shower which transforms the stream of water into a beautiful waterfall of light." "Powerful electric energy for LED, which is generated by waterpower. Does not require any battery or electrical power supply." Colour-by-temperature: "Water glows Red when temperature is above 42 C"; "Water glows Purple when temperature is between 32-42 C". (Degree symbols printed as ºC.)

**Shower Panels (p259):** "Jaquar's Shower Panels make showering much more convenient, since they can offer all the capability of a shower stall, even in a small bathroom." Models with printed sizes: Curve Neo 1500 x 230 mm (marked NEW in the catalogue); Selfy 1600 x 200 mm; Shinny 1600 x 200 mm *(printed "SHINNY" - possibly "Shiny", kept)*; Glassy White 1400 x 250 mm; Glassy Black 1400 x 250 mm; Blak 1500 x 200 mm *(printed "BLAK", kept)*; Infinity White 1500 x 200 mm; Infinity Black 1500 x 200 mm; Projects 1400 x 200 mm; My Home EP / My Home GB 1450 x 175 mm. Repeated disclaimer on panel pages: "Pictures shown are for illustration purposes only".

**MISSING vs the live site:** No Octane showers anywhere in this catalogue, and no shower enclosures at all. The live showers/octane-showers and the whole shower-enclosures category must continue to be fed from uae.jaquar.com material.

## 6. Wellness  (pages 282-357)

The PDF's Wellness banner: "Stimulating Invigorating Restorative" (p282).

### Whirlpools - category features, verbatim (pp285-287)
- "Brilliant surface of acrylic; Antibac(R) hygienic surface; Increased chemical resistance; High break strength; Long life; Excellent design possibilities; Easyglide(R) layer; Low specific weight; Recyclable; Repairable" (p286).
- Bathtub layers (p286): "12 MM" three-layer build - "Special transparent acrylic top layer", "Special Polymer layer", "Polyurethane layer". "Special transparent acrylic top layer has a high chemical resistance to: cleaning agents, perfumed foams, household detergents, body care products. It prevents surface defects, crazing/cracking, decolouration and colour fading."
- Safety Vacuum Breaker (p287): "In case of blockage in the suction point the motor switches off automatically."
- No Bacteria Formation: "The water pipes connect via Y-shaped housings, creating a bow which ensures the complete drainage of water."
- Environment Friendly: "pipes are made from special Food Grade PVC... anti bacterial coating on the inner side".
- 3 Tier Test: "functional test with hot water after which it is tested on a European test machine for earthing resistance, insulation test and a high voltage test."
- Water Jets, Airpool Jet, PMMA Sheet ("highest grade of PMMA acrylic sheets"), Air Suction Valve - full paragraphs on p287.

**CONTRADICTION FLAG (gold):** the site currently states "1.4 HP pumps" as a blanket whirlpool fact. This sheet CONTRADICTS that for Fonte 1800X800, Fusion 1400X1400, Fonte 1500x750 and Fonte 1700x750, which print 1.0HP / 1HP pumps. The blanket claim must become per-model.
**CONFIRMATION:** slim and flat water jets, chromotherapy, digital controls, SS202 frames, PMMA acrylic confirmed across models.

### Spas (pages 319-334)
Section header prints "jaquar.org.uk | Page - 259" (p319) - a UK-domain artifact inside the global catalogue, flagged. Every spa page carries "Pictures shown are for illustration purposes only".

Models with printed sizes (name, size, seats): HYDROZONE 5900 x 2300 x 1300 (5 Seater); AQUAGYM MAX EXTREME 4490 x 2310 x 1300 (4 Seater); AQUA SWIM 4500 x 2300 x 1300; PALLADIUM 3520 x 2290 x 920 (9 Seater); XENON 2300 x 2300 x 920 (6 Seater); POLARIS SPA 2200X2200X900 (6 Seater); NIRWANA 2150 x 2150 x 920 (5 Seater); BREVA SPA 2000X2000X900 (5 Seater); MERCURY 2000 x 2000 x 920 (5 Seater); NEON 2060 x 1690 x 820 (3 Seater); NUOVO SPA 1950X1400X75 (2 Seater) *(depth printed as 75 - likely 750, kept and flagged)*; AVANTE TOP 1870 x 1280 x 740 (2 Seater); GEMINI 2100 x 1100 x 740 (2 Seater); FISHER 2 1650 x 1650 x 740 (2 Seater).

The full "Jaquar Spas product features sheet" (p334) is a spec goldmine - key rows, exactly as printed: Dry Weight (Hydrozone 1382kg, Gemini 167kg, Neon 254kg, Xenon 334kg, Mercury 292kg, Palladium 490kg, Aquagym Max Extreme 1052kg, Aqua swim 696kg, Fisher 2 220kg, Nirvana 320kg, Avante 179kg, Polaris 420kg, Breva 350kg, Nuovo 215kg); Filled Weight (9902kg / 467kg / 854kg / 1534kg / 1292kg / 4090kg / 9152kg / 7494kg / 620kg / 1320kg / 779kg / 1920kg / 1380kg / 735kg); Water Capacity (Hydrozone "Spa 1530 ltr, Swim 7030ltr", Gemini 300 litres, Neon 600 litres, Xenon 1200 litres, Mercury 1000 litres, Palladium 3600litres, Aquagym Max 8100 litres, Aqua swim 6800 litres, Fisher 2 400 litres, Nirvana 1000 litres, Avante 600 litres, Polaris 1500 litres, Breva 1030 litres, Nuovo 520 litres); Spa Controller (SV4 + SV2, Gecko, SV2, SV2, SV2, SV3, SV4, SV M2, SV2, SV2, SV2, Balboa, Balboa, Balboa); Jet Pump(s) (e.g. Hydrozone "2.5hp x 4, 2.5hp x 1 or (2.5 hp x 5)"); Variable Output Heater (6 KW max / 3 KW max on Hydrozone; 1.3 KW Gemini; 3 KW most models; 6KW Palladium and Aquagym Max); Electrical Requirements (Max 60 amps Hydrozone down to 13 amp Nuovo); full jet-count matrix (5" directional, 5" spinning, 3" directional, 3" spinning, 2" directional, 2" spinning, Air Jet, Water Cascade, Swim, Volcano, Laminar); feature checkmarks for Set & Forget Control System, Thermo bond 4 layer shell construction, High-density PU insulation foam on shell: 25mm (Balboa models only), Reinforced Foot Wall, Child Seats & Wine Cooler (Xenon only), Programmable Circulation Pump, Twin Accu-Pressure Seats, Hydrotherapy Recliners, Water Spout (Xenon only), Dual Controllers, Programmable Multi-Colour Spa Light, Moulded Headrests, Water Cascade, Adjustable Aromatherapy, Lockable Hard Cover (Thermic Cover), Illuminated Topside Controls, Cup holder illumination, Individual Seat Pressure. Reproduce from p334 verbatim when building the spa pages; the transcription above preserves the printed values including inconsistent unit spellings ("3600litres", "2.5 x 1hp").

### Water Heaters (pages 343-351)
Category line: "Gas | Electric | Instant" (p343). "Why Jaquar Water Heaters - Water heaters that gives you more." (p344) *(printed "gives", kept)*. Page 345 carries a "How to select your water heater on the basis of capacity" guide - FAQ material. Ranges with printed capacities: INSTANT ELECTRIC "Available in 1/3 litres"; ELENA PRIME (Manual) "Available in 6/10/15/25 litres"; ELENA MANUAL - HORIZONTAL "Available in 15/25 litres"; ERICA VERTICAL (Digital); VERSA MANUAL - VERTICAL (VME) "Available in 30/50/80/100 litres"; VERNA FLOOR MOUNTING (Manual) "Available in 200/300/400/500 litres".

Hot Water Solution warranty matrix (p360): columns Tank / Heating Element / Other spare part / Compressor & sealed system: Jaquar - Instant 5/2/2/NA; Jaquar - Storage upto 100L 7/4/2/NA; Jaquar - Storage above 100L 3/3/3/NA; Jaquar - Tank Less NA/3/1/NA; Jaquar - Heat pump 4/2/1/2; Jaquar - Gas geyser 1/NA/1/NA; Essco - Instant & storage upto 100L 5/2/2/NA.

Footnote: "Our warranty does not cover certain product consumables like batteries or fragile items like glass".

**CONFIRMATION AND SHARPENING of the site's "10-year warranty" fact:** 10 years applies to faucets (metal parts and braided hose), flush valves and sanitaryware ceramic bodies. It does NOT apply to colour-finish faucets (5), sensor faucets (5), wellness products (2-10 tiered), or water heaters (tiered). Blanket "10-year warranty on everything" copy must be avoided.

Maintenance guidelines (p360, condensed quotes): "Clean the product with water-soaked soft towel or bathing soap solution once in a day or two"; "Don't use any type of cleaning/detergent agents which contain acids or chemicals such as flush cleaner, tile cleaner etc."; "Don't use salt or vinegar-based cleaning agents"; "Special color combination products should not be rubbed by Emery paper/cleaning powder"; hardness of water "should not exceed 300-400ppm"; plus Water Scaling, Acid Fumes, Scratching while cleaning, and Atmospheric conditions (coastal salt air) advisories, and sanitaryware handling: "The wash basin and water closet are heavy and it is recommended that two persons hold it while it is being fixed."

Warranty terms and conditions (p361): "Warranty is offered by Jaquar & Company Pvt. Ltd..." with the closing line: "Jaquar has an ongoing policy of design, development and improvement and thus reserves the right to modify specification and prices without any prior notice."

## 8. Certifications

As printed on pp18-19, each with its printed description: ZNAK-B ("collective guarantee trademark... safe, up-to-date, and environment-friendly"); SASO ("Saudi Arabia certificate of Conformity... necessary to clear customs when exporting to Saudi Arabia"); IKRAM QA (ISO/IEC 17065 product certification); DVGW (German Technical and Scientific Association for Gas and Water); TISI (Thai Industrial Standards Institute); PZH (National Institute of Hygiene, Poland); ESMA ("Emirates Authority for Standardization & Metrology" - the UAE-relevant mark, quote on p19); WRAS ("The Water Regulations Approval Scheme is an independent UK certification body for plumbing products"); WELS (Mandatory Water Efficiency Labeling Scheme, PUB Singapore); ISO 9001 (Quality Management System); SABS (South Africa); KC (Korea Certification); KIWA (European laws and regulations); CE (European Economic Area); SONCAP (Nigerian Customs Services). ESMA and WRAS CONFIRM facts already on the site; the rest EXTEND the certification story for the schema/entity section.

## 9. Technical specification tables

The catalogue's graphical spec tables are transcribed in their sections above: Whirlpools specification sheet and Warranty Table (pp300-301, section 6); Spas product features sheet (p334, section 6); Warranty Information and Hot Water Solution matrix (p360, section 7); shower shapes/sizes and registered design numbers (p246, section 5); flushing system volumes 3/6 L adjustable to 2/4 L (p225, section 3 - CONFIRMS the site's 3/6 L fact and EXTENDS it with the 2/4 L adjustment). Flow rates are printed per-faucet in the product tables (all "at 3 bar pressure"). Glossary of product terminology is printed on p359 (including "Adjustable Exposed Shower Pipe" and other definitions) - useful for FAQ schema.


## Notes for website content generation

1. NEVER invent prices. This catalogue prints none. Any price shown on the site must come from a separate Lapiz/GCBM price source.
2. NEVER present the 10-year warranty as blanket. Use the per-category table in section 7. Colour-finish faucets carry 5 years, not 10 - a strong honest-FAQ differentiator.
3. The site's "1.4 HP pumps" whirlpool claim is contradicted for four Fonte/Fusion models (1.0HP/1HP). Make pump rating per-model from the section 6 sheet.
4. This PDF has NO shower enclosures and NO Octane showers. Do not let the enrichment pass overwrite or orphan those live pages; they remain sourced from uae.jaquar.com.
5. Fusion vs fusion-prime slug conflict: PDF prints FUSION (FUS- SKUs) only. Site Claude must decide alias-or-split before merging the faucets/fusion folder.
6. Spelling landmines kept verbatim and flagged inline: "Sourat" (p29), "SHINNY" and "BLAK" panels (pp262, 265), "healing Chromotherapy light" (p247), "Sanitazation" (p300), "PMaMA" (p301), "180X800" Alive size (p300), Nuovo Spa depth "75" (p330), warranty "Nill" (p360), Rendezvous finish example citing CTL code (p104), page 62 header printed LAGUNA over Kubix SKUs, pages 68-69 headed OPAL not OPAL PRIME.
7. Quotable statistics for entity/schema: 65+ years, 55+ countries, 8 plants, 330,000 sq. m, 3.3 million bathrooms/year, 45.9 million bath fittings/year, 24.6 million lighting products/year, 2.59MW solar, 100,000 litres rainwater recycled daily, LEED Platinum HQ, first Indian Red Dot winner. Note the older figures on p11 (39 million fittings, 25 million lighting) differ from p13 (45.9m, 24.6m) - the timeline page vs the brand page disagree; prefer p13 as current, flag internally.
8. EKO is "For projects only" - present it under a projects/contract framing, never as retail stock.
9. Finish-code mechanism (replace the middle code) is uniform across ranges and is the backbone for the SKU decoder: base SKUs in the tables + finish cards per collection give the full orderable matrix. Laguna adds dual-tone codes BBC/BGM/GMG; Rendezvous is CHR/GLD only; Cloud trims come in 6 finishes.
10. Every finish card carries "The finishes depicted here are only indicative and may differ from that on the actual product" and wellness/panel/spa pages carry "Pictures shown are for illustration purposes only" - keep these disclaimers on spec-heavy pages for accuracy.
11. Image pack realities: source crops are ~150 dpi web-PDF assets (200-500 px typical). They are correct for identification and for the Higgsfield upscale pass, but MUST NOT ship raw at hero sizes. Whirlpool, bathtub, spa, sauna, panel and steam images are installed-scene shots, not clean cutouts - they belong on lifestyle-style cards; faucet/sanitaryware/shower crops are clean-background cutouts suitable for Marble White / Onyx Green product cards.
12. Sensor faucets split battery vs 9v-transformer operation; flushing frames take 400 kg, legs adjust 0-200 mm, inlet/outlet fixed at 135 mm centres; i-Flush 32 mm works from 0.8 bar - all strong FAQ/rich-result facts with page refs.
13. The typographic litre glyph in flow rates was normalized to "l/min"; curly quotes normalized straight; no em or en dashes exist anywhere in this file or the csv (owner law).
14. index.csv is the single source of truth for the image pack: every file in the folders appears in it, every row's file exists, categories/collections use the site slugs where live and marked-NEW slugs otherwise.
